) AS [s0] ON [s].[RoleId0] = [s0].[RoleId]

FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [e].[EmployeeId], [e].[Department], [e].[Email], [e].[FirstName], [e].[LastName], [e].[ManagerId], [e].[PasswordHash], [e].[RoleId], [e].[Salary], [e].[UserName], [r].[RoleId] AS [RoleId0], [r].[Name]
    FROM [Employees] AS [e]
    INNER JOIN [Roles] AS [r] ON [e].[RoleId] = [r].[RoleId]
    WHERE [e].[UserName] = @__username_0
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [r0].[RoleId], [r0].[PermissionId], [p].[PermissionId] AS [PermissionId0], [p].[PermissionName]
    FROM [RolePermissions] AS [r0]
    INNER JOIN [Permissions] AS [p] ON [r0].[PermissionId] = [p].[PermissionId]
) AS [s0] ON [s].[RoleId0] = [s0].[RoleId]
ORDER BY [s].[EmployeeId], [s].[RoleId0], [s0].[RoleId], [s0].[PermissionId]
